# Rule verdicts on the 14 days (one line per restricted book)

# Restricted books against their free parents, trade by trade

A restricted book is its parent plus one rule. Matching each trade by trigger second and contract: **kept** = the parent took it and so did the restricted book; **removed** = the parent took it, the rule said no; **extra** = the restricted book took it and the parent did not (the parent was busy in a trade the rule had removed, so a later trigger that the parent missed was free for the restricted book). A rule earns its place only if the removed trades lose and the extra trades do not undo that.

## c20_9x_r002  ←  c20_9x  via R-002: Normal days: trend followers only after a trending last hour

Condition: on a normal_day, enter only if ER60 >= 0.35 and DIR60 = d at the trigger second; otherwise (including before 10:15 and whenever ER60 is undefined) no entry.

| group | trades | wins | losses | win % | mean % | P&L ₹ | losing days |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| parent (free) c20_9x | 81 | 43 | 38 | 53% | +8.6 | +1,004,809 | 4 |
| restricted c20_9x_r002 | 41 | 23 | 18 | 56% | +6.2 | +530,257 | 1 |
| kept (both took) | 41 | 23 | 18 | 56% | +6.2 | +530,257 | 1 |
| removed by the rule | 40 | 20 | 20 | 50% | +11.0 | +474,552 | 3 |
| extra (restricted only) | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0% | +0.0 | +0 | 0 |

**Reading:** removed trades were WINNERS on balance (₹+474,552, 50% winners); net effect of the rule: ₹-474,552 against the parent.

# The losing trades of the restricted books

Every losing trade of every restricted book: when, what, the rule, exit reason, hold, return. `kept` means the parent took the same trade (so the loss is the family's, not the rule's); `extra` means the rule's removal of an earlier trade exposed the restricted book to this one.
